     Its expectation conditional on C is ∆C (b). The first difference cancels the common
8                                                                                                         8
     state cost exactly. The remaining terms retain a separately propagated state differ-
9                                                                                                         9
     ence rather than subtracting independently widened state boxes.
10                                                                                                        10
       For example, δ = Y b − Y a = ((b − a)/2, (b − a)/4). The bilinear difference satisfies
11                                                                                                        11

12                                   Y1b Y2b − Y1a Y2a = δ1 Y2b + Y1a δ2 .                                12

13                                                                                                        13
     This identity gives the difference of the next mean states, including the separate
14                                                                                                        14
     term B − A. The square identity u2 − v2 = (u − v)(u + v) and the quartic identity
15                                                                                                        15
     u4 − v4 = (u − v)(u + v)(u2 + v2 ) preserve analogous cancellation in costs. For the
16                                                                                                        16
     shortage term, whenever an interval encloses d = u − v,
17                                                                                                        17

18                                                                                                        18
                        u+ − v+ ∈ ([u]+ − [v]+ ) ∩ [min{0, d}, max{0, d}].
19                                                                                                        19

20   Multiplication by an enclosure of u+ + v+ encloses the difference of their squares. 20
21   An empty intersection stops verification.                                                            21

22                                                                                                        22
       P ROPOSITION 36—Complete-bin enclosure: Partition each initial cell into r2 equal
23                                                                                                        23
     rectangles and the first innovation into q equal bins, with dyadic r, q. Evaluate (86) on
24                                                                                                        24
     every complete subbox by outward interval operations. On each next-state rectangle, enclose
25                                                                                                        25
     the fixed actor by the minimum and maximum of its action indices over every intersected
26                                                                                                        26
     acquired cell, including boundary cells. Let [ls , us ] be the resulting paired interval on subbox
27                                                                                                        27
     s, intersected with the valid signed support [− H, H ]. Then
28                                                                                                        28
                                      1                  1
                                      2 ∑ s
29                                         l ≤ ∆C (b ) ≤ 2 ∑ us .                                         29

30
                                     r q s              r q s                                             30

31   The hull of two independently valid interval implementations is valid. Intersecting indepen- 31
32   dently valid integrated bands across refinement levels is valid as well.                             32
                              NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                                  131

1      P ROOF : The transition and centered identities are algebraic identities for every                 1

2    state and innovation in the subbox. The rectangle extrema contain the actual actor                   2

3    action even when the rectangle crosses an actor discontinuity. Interval inclusion,                   3

4    propagated through each arithmetic operation and the valid intersections, therefore                  4

5    contains (86) pointwise. Equation (85) accounts for the remaining innovation under                   5

6    its actual continuous marginal law. All subboxes have exact conditional probability                  6

7    1/(r2 q), so integration preserves the endpoint inequalities. Taking a hull preserves                7

8    inclusion, as does intersecting two bands that separately contain the same integral. 8
9                                                                                              Q.E.D.     9

10                                                                                                        10

11     The two deposited implementations do not import each other’s interval, transi- 11
12   tion, cost, partition or actor routines. The binary64 implementation uses outward                    12

13   next-representable-number operations and a precomputed rectangle-extremum                            13

14   table. The integer implementation uses Python-integer endpoints in units 2−48 ,                      14

15   directed integer division, expanded cost polynomials and direct enumeration of                       15

16   distinct acquisition rectangles. Their endpoint streams must overlap on every sub- 16
17   box. A separately written rational trajectory calculation checks four deterministic                  17

18   midpoint trajectories per action and rung. Those checks are regressions; the proof                   18

19   concerns every point in each box, not only its midpoint.                                             19

20     The complete records retain exact integer endpoint sums, their common denomi- 20
21   nator, the digest of all four independently computed endpoint streams, the largest                   21

22   endpoint discrepancy and the number of processed boxes. They do not claim to                         22

23   deposit an additional random sample or to count the two arithmetic implementa- 23
24   tions as independent statistical observations. Full replay recomputes those endpoint                 24

25   streams, not only the saved sums.                                                                    25

26                                                                                                        26

27                                                                                                        27
                             AP.2. Refinement and resource requirements
28                                                                                                        28

29     P ROPOSITION 37—Refinement with a discontinuous acquired actor: In the origi- 29
30   nal two-date model, fix the finite acquired actor, initial cell and feasible first-action pair. In   30

31   exact interval arithmetic, the integrated width in Proposition 36 tends to zero as r, q → ∞          31

32   along dyadic partitions. The same conclusion holds with outward arithmetic whose uni- 32
     132

1    form propagated padding tends to zero. The executed fixed-precision calculation retains its     1

2    nonzero numerical width rather than invoking this limiting statement.                           2

3      If P alternatives remain at the largest qualification tolerance, a level uses exactly Pr2 q   3

4    paired subboxes in each implementation. Its binary64 actor tables occupy O(n4 ) entries and     4

5    the direct preprocessing used here takes at most O(n6 ) operations. After that preprocessing    5

6    each rectangle query is constant work. The independent enumerator instead stores the n2         6

7    actor indices, groups queried rectangles by sorting their codes, and can inspect up to n2       7

8    indices per distinct rectangle. Its conservative worst-case work is therefore                   8

9                                                                                                  9
                                             2   2         2
                                       O Pr q [n + log(r q)] .
10                                                                                                   10

11                                                                                                   11
     Processing one alternative at a time uses O(n4 + r2 q) numerical working storage, apart
12                                                                                                   12
     from retained integrated records.
13                                                                                                   13

14     P ROOF : For a fixed first action, each next-state coordinate is a polynomial of              14

15   the initial state plus either W or −W. For any terminal acquisition boundary Yj =               15

16   k/n, its preimage has conditional innovation measure zero for every initial state,              16

17   because the coefficient of W is nonzero. There are finitely many boundaries and two             17

18   coupled trajectories. Fubini’s theorem therefore gives a measure-zero union of their            18

19   preimages in the integration domain.                                                            19

20     At every other interior point, sufficiently fine boxes intersect only the fixed               20

21   actor cell containing each next state. On such boxes, (86) is a finite composition of           21

22   continuous polynomial and positive-part operations with constant actor actions. 22
23   Its exact interval width tends to zero. The support intersection bounds all widths              23

24   by 2H. Boundary sets of the initial partition also have measure zero. Dominated                 24

25   convergence now gives convergence of the integrated width. Vanishing outward                    25

26   padding preserves the argument. It does not justify removing the actual fixed- 26
27   precision remainder from a finite calculation.                                                  27

28     There are r2 q subboxes per action and P retained actions. The direct binary64                28

29   precomputation enumerates O(n4 ) rectangles and scans at most n2 entries each. A                29

30   stored rectangle extremum then uses its four indices. For m = r2 q boxes, the inde- 30
31   pendent algorithm spends O(m log m) work grouping codes and at most O(mn2 )                     31

32   work enumerating their actor indices. Finally, keeping only one alternative’s in- 32
                            NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                           133

1    terval arrays at a time gives the stated working storage. Summing the displayed            1

2    counts over every executed rung, rather than only the last one, gives the complete         2

3    refinement work.                                                                 Q.E.D.    3

4                                                                                               4

5                                                                                               5

6      These are counts for the actual two-date procedure. They are not a reduction of          6

7    the retained vector recursion’s horizon growth, and table bytes are not substituted        7

8    for process peak memory. The completed R83 recovery records its verification               8

9    clock and peak resident memory separately from the interrupted R82 phase clocks. 9
10   Development pilots informed the dyadic resolution before the source freeze; the            10

11   computation is described as deterministic verification rather than a prospectively         11

12   selected new economic experiment.                                                          12

13                                                                                              13

14                                                                                              14

15                   AP.3. A classwide adoption decision on the original event                  15

16                                                                                              16

17     Let the signed optimizer-sublevel interval for J π j − J πk be [ L, U ] after applying   17

18   (21). If deployment of class k rather than class j has an additional total charge τ, the   18

19   net advantage of k belongs to [ L − τ, U − τ ] for every allowed pair of readouts on the   19

20   same original event. Thus τ < L supports k uniformly and τ > U supports the oppo- 20
21   site decision uniformly. The intermediate region remains unresolved. This statement        21

22   prices construction, verification and installation only when their conversion into         22

23   economic cost units is explicitly supplied. A measured second of verification is not       23

24   itself such a price.                                                                       24

25     The extension uses a deterministic outer set of possible acquired actions. It permits    25

26   the qualification tolerance or the comparison to be selected after observing the           26

27   original simultaneous cost event, provided the original attained controllers and           27

28   event are not changed and the stated deterministic enclosures hold. There is no new        28

29   probability expenditure over an uncountable set of readouts. Conversely, a newly           29

30   fitted hidden-feature model, a changed terminal actor, altered transition law or new       30

31   statistical estimand is not covered merely because its displayed objective resembles       31

32   the one here.                                                                              32
     134

1                              AP.4. Complete refinement catalogue                           1

2      The tables retain all four calibrated constructions, all three objective allowances   2

3    and all four refinement rungs. ReLU and Bernstein are each evaluated at both            3

4    resolutions, including the coarse ReLU construction whose exact-optimizer action        4
     certificate remains partly unresolved. The actionwise radii are compared with their
5                                                                                            5
     binary uncertified-mass bounds. Every calibrated pair and both own-controller
6                                                                                            6
     references are retained in the machine-readable records; no unresolved or adverse
7    entry is removed.                                                                       7

19              APPENDIX AQ: E XACT INVERSION , BOUNDARY CHECKS AND                                           19

20                                   SOURCE - QUALIFIED RECOVERY                                              20

21                     AQ.1. A finite algorithm for the entire tolerance interval                             21

22                                                                                                            22
       For one construction, let P be the number of integrated nonreference actions and
23                                                                                                            23
     N the number of acquisition cells. Sort the action events by DCb − U. Events with
24                                                                                                            24
     nonpositive threshold are active at zero. Maintain the lowest lower endpoint and
25                                                                                                            25
     highest upper endpoint on each cell, initially including the exact reference interval
26                                                                                                            26
     [0, 0]. When an event is activated, update those two extrema and their probability-
27                                                                                                            27
     weighted sums. Equal thresholds are processed together. The resulting records
28                                                                                                            28
     contain every knot, the two class endpoints, the number of admitted alternatives,
29                                                                                                            29
     and the open or closed status of the stratum’s right endpoint.
30                                                                                                            30

31     P ROPOSITION 38—Exact finite inversion: Suppose the switch bounds, attained ob- 31
32   jectives, cell masses and integrated endpoints are rational. The complete class band is                  32
     140

1    computed with O( P log P + N ) comparisons and arithmetic operations after integration,      1

2    using O( P + N ) storage. Let K j , Kk denote the numbers of tolerance strata for two con- 2
3    structions. For a fixed replacement charge or equivalence margin, the sufficient region in   3

4    Theorem 7 can be represented by at most K j horizontal strips and found using at most        4

5    K j + Kk endpoint-predicate evaluations. The same bound applies to a certified ordering in   5

6    the opposite direction.                                                                      6

7                                                                                                 7
       P ROOF : Sorting costs O( P log P) comparisons. Each event changes at most two
8                                                                                                 8
     cell extrema and two weighted sums; reference initialization takes O( N ) work.
9                                                                                                 9
     Storing events, cells and resulting strata uses the stated memory. These counts
10                                                                                                10
     exclude the already paid action integration, and rational bit complexity depends on
11                                                                                                11
     the input denominators.
12                                                                                                12
       Order both stratum lists from smaller to larger tolerance. Start at the largest
13                                                                                                13
     second-coordinate stratum. For the current first-coordinate stratum, decrease the
14                                                                                                14
     second index until the desired endpoint test holds or the index is exhausted. Record
15                                                                                                15
     the resulting maximal admissible second-coordinate prefix. Move to the next first-
16                                                                                                16
     coordinate stratum and continue from the same second index. Monotonicity implies
17                                                                                                17
     that this index never needs to increase. Each successful test advances the first
18                                                                                                18
     coordinate, and each failed test decreases the second coordinate. There are at most
19                                                                                                19
     K j + Kk tests. Each recorded strip has the first stratum’s exact endpoint convention
20                                                                                                20
     and the upper boundary convention of its last admitted second stratum. Exhaustion
21                                                                                                21
     leaves no later feasible strip. Thus the algorithm returns precisely the largest region
22                                                                                                22
     certified by these fixed endpoint tests, not merely an inner grid of isolated tolerance
23                                                                                                23
     points.                                                                            Q.E.D.
24                                                                                                24

25     The implementation also enumerates the finite product grid independently and               25

26   checks every returned and excluded stratum pair. Separately, it rebuilds the possible- 26
27   action set directly from the original switch predicates at every knot, every adjacent- 27
28   stratum midpoint and the final endpoint. This check does not reuse the incremental           28

29   cell-extremum update. It includes negative and repeated switch thresholds, a switch          29

30   at the maximum allowed tolerance, and strict sign or equivalence tests at equality.          30

31     An economic margin and an objective excess are not interchangeable numbers. 31
32   The plotted or tabulated tolerance region is an implication from one to the other            32
                           NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                           141

1    for the specified construction. It neither assumes that the nonlinear ReLU and            1

2    Bernstein classes coincide nor attributes a larger allowable objective excess to          2

3    universally better numerical conditioning. A valid stopping certificate is sufficient     3

4    for an individual output; it is not a probability that a randomly initialized algorithm   4

5    attains that certificate within a given time.                                             5

6                                                                                              6

7                        AQ.2. Finite numerical and statistical domains                        7

8                                                                                              8
       The integrated catalogue is complete up to e j = 1/4096. Actions whose switch
9                                                                                              9
     lower bound exceeds Uj + e j cannot be selected within that objective sublevel and
10                                                                                             10
     require no economic integration for this result. The calculation does not extrapolate
11                                                                                             11
     the band beyond e j . Each class has its original labels, feature map, coefficient box,
12                                                                                             12
     acquisition grid, robust action menu and fixed terminal actor. Changing any of these
13                                                                                             13
     objects requires new qualification and, where relevant, a new integration account.
14                                                                                             14
       There is no new empirical-Bernstein event in this inversion. The original nine-
15                                                                                             15
     controller simultaneous event supplies the attained marginal and paired costs.
16                                                                                             16
     The actionwise bounds extend it deterministically to all admissible readouts and
17                                                                                             17
     tolerances. A data-selected comparison or stopping tolerance is covered because
18                                                                                             18
     the event already implies every stated comparison. An unlisted controller or an
19                                                                                             19
     additional statistical estimand does not acquire coverage merely by receiving a
20                                                                                             20
     similar display name.
21                                                                                             21
       For a common tolerance the sufficient region is an initial interval, possibly empty
22                                                                                             22
     or the entire declared domain. When a switch destroys the endpoint test, its exact
23                                                                                             23
     knot is the supremum and is excluded. Decimal summaries must therefore not be
24                                                                                             24
     used as executable stopping thresholds. The deposited rational boundaries and
25                                                                                             25
     their inclusion flags are authoritative. A deployment fee is handled by comparing
26                                                                                             26
     the lower gain endpoint with the supplied fee, not by converting a measured second
27                                                                                             27
     into a monetary amount without a declared exchange rate.
28                                                                                             28

5    identity map, with construction and callable-actor hashes bound to the existing cost      5

6    record.                                                                                   6

30   controller is refitted, and the original-law cost observations are unchanged. The ad- 30

32   by the exact optimization barriers. It therefore answers a different reliability ques- 32
     144

1    tion from whether one numerical fitting attempt returned its displayed coefficient                        1

2    vector.                                                                                                   2

3      The objective allowances are 0, 1/65536 and 1/4096. At the largest allowance,                           3

4    the complete 20,480-switch catalogue leaves 976 alternative actions for economic                          4

5    evaluation: 832 for coarse ReLU, nine for coarse Bernstein, 78 for fine ReLU                              5

6    and 57 for fine Bernstein. Each is evaluated on all four dyadic levels (r, q) =                           6

7    (2, 8), (4, 16), (8, 32), (16, 64), where r subdivides each initial-cell coordinate and                   7

8    q subdivides the first innovation. No action is discarded because its cost effect is                      8

9    adverse or its switch bound is uninformative. Both whole-bin arithmetic implemen- 9
10   tations evaluate every level, and the final innovation retains its exact conditional                      10

11   expectation.                                                                                              11

27     The comparison concerns all readouts in the two attained-objective sublevels,                           27

28   including every 1/65536-optimal readout of their respective fixed-label problems. 28
29   It does not require the same fitted coefficients or complete action agreement. By                         29

30   contrast, an arbitrary fitting attempt with no verified objective guarantee does not                      30

31   enter the class merely because it returns a feasible policy. The original fallback and                    31

32   fitting-warning results retain their separate interpretation.                                             32
                            NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                         145

1      For an additional Bernstein deployment charge τ, subtracting τ from the two            1

2    endpoints gives the uniform net-benefit interval for choosing Bernstein over ReLU. 2
3    A charge below a positive lower endpoint is covered by the classwide policy-cost         3

4    saving; a charge above the upper endpoint favors the opposite decision. The inter- 4
5    mediate region is unresolved. The comparison uses normalized model costs and             5

6    an explicitly supplied charge, rather than an invented processor price or empirical      6

7    installation calibration.                                                                7

8      The complete numerical appendix includes all 48 model–allowance–level bands,           8

9    all 72 pairwise transfers and all 24 final-level comparisons with the zero or cor- 9
10   responding legacy controller. It retains the coarse ReLU sublevels and the larger- 10
11   allowance outcomes alongside the fine-grid comparison. The cellwise extrema are          11

12   outer bounds: separately possible actions need not be jointly selectable by a single     12

13   coefficient vector. Their residual width is not characterized as a population fitting    13

14   error.                                                                                   14

15     The completed recovery reevaluates 18,270,720 paired boxes per arithmetic imple- 15
16   mentation and verifies 15,616 exact rational midpoint differences. Its independently     16

17   recorded time through the verification calculations is 515.246 seconds; peak resident    17

18   memory is 88,652 KiB. The failed R82 run retains its own partial integration-phase       18

19   clocks and exception record. This recovery is not a new policy-cost sample. The          19

20   completed R83 recovery accounts for all refinement levels, both arithmetic im- 20
21   plementations and rational checks; its output-accounting boundary is recorded            21

22   explicitly. Subsequent replay has separate clocks. The interrupted R82 phase clocks      22

23   are preserved, not replaced or promoted to a missing complete-service clock. The         23

24   computation adds neither an independent cost-observation row nor a new success- 24
25   frequency sample. Development pilots informed the deterministic refinement reso- 25
26   lution before its source freeze; it is not presented as a newly randomized prospective   26

27   economic experiment. The unchanged prospective R80 statistical family and the            27

28   new deterministic integration together support the classwide statements on their         28

29   original event.                                                                          29

31   zi , one unit of mass per acquisition cell, and zi + z j ≤ 1 for each verified incom- 31
32   patible pair. Given nonnegative rational pair multipliers λij and weights wi , the      32
     148

1    construction                                                                              1

                                                                       
4                                                                                              4

5                                                                                              5

6                                                                                              6

       The pair multipliers are rounded to nonnegative dyadic rationals. Every remain-
9                                                                                              9

15                                                                                             15
                              AS.4. Why both refinements matter
16                                                                                             16

17     For three action scores 0, −1, −2, the second action defeats the reference score        17

18   zero but cannot win the full menu. A reference-only barrier can therefore remain          18

19   inconclusive even though the complete winner event is empty. This is a distinction        19

20   about the implemented argmin, not a change in the economic action set.                    20

21     For the shared-coefficient restriction, take θ ∈ [−1, 1] and two cells. An adverse      21

22   action in the first cell requires θ ≥ 1; one in the second requires θ ≤ −1. Both          22

23   events are separately feasible but their intersection is empty. If each adverse action    23

24   contributes 1/2 to aggregate cost, independent cell bounds allow a cost increase          24

25   of one. The compatible bound is 1/2, and it is attained by one endpoint of the            25

26   parameter interval. This supplies a strict example for the improvement claimed by         26

27   the theorem.                                                                              27

28     Pair restrictions need not characterize every feasible policy. With three unit- 28
29   weight alternatives and all three pair conflicts, an assignment relaxation permits        29

30   mass 1/2 on each alternative, even though a zero–one assignment can select at most        30

31   one. The corresponding fractional support can therefore be larger than the true           31

32   worst cost. More generally, pairwise intersection does not imply joint intersection       32
                           NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                         149

1    of all winner regions. The manuscript consequently calls the computed object an         1

2    enclosure, not the exact attainable policy set or the exact worst optimizer output. 2
3    Additional certified joint events may strengthen it without invalidating any existing   3

4    bound.                                                                                  4

5                                                                                            5

6                         AS.5. Work, storage and continuation scope                         6

7                                                                                            7
       Let P be the number of admitted alternatives, K the number of declared joint
8                                                                                            8
     events, m the maximum menu size, d the readout dimension, N the number of cells,
9                                                                                            9
     and E the number of retained pair cuts at a tolerance. A singleton event adds at most
10                                                                                           10
     m − 1 rows and a pair event at most 2(m − 1) rows to the same fixed qualification
11                                                                                           11
     problem. The experiment executes exactly P + K qualification problems. It does
12                                                                                           12
     not claim a dimension-independent bound on an LP solver’s arithmetic or bit
13                                                                                           13
     complexity.
14                                                                                           14
       Once multipliers have been obtained, checking a qualification certificate takes
15
     a number of rational operations linear in its feature, derivative and event coeffi- 15
16                                                                                           16
     cients. Checking one assignment dual takes O( P + N + E) rational operations and
17                                                                                           17
     storage linear in its retained variables, cells and cuts. Constructing and solving
18                                                                                           18
     the assignment LP is separate work. The executed pair budget limits qualification
19
     work without assuming that unchecked intersections are feasible or infeasible. Pre- 19
20                                                                                           20
     viously purchased conditional integration remains an explicit prerequisite, not free
21                                                                                           21
     computation.
22                                                                                           22
       The identity transferring conditional contrasts to aggregate policy cost holds
23                                                                                           23
     because the policies share the stated future controller and only their first acquired
24
     action varies. A longer horizon can use the same identity when its entire fixed- 24
25                                                                                           25
     continuation conditional cost is enclosed. Jointly refitting that future controller
26                                                                                           26
     changes the estimand and requires another qualification argument. The results
27                                                                                           27
     therefore do not claim that the present pair graph certifies arbitrary changes at
28                                                                                           28
     every date or regenerates the large vector-control descendant tree.

22     Equivalence requires the exact rational interval to be strictly inside the predeclared margin. It is   22

23   distinct from a signed ranking; both can hold for a small nonzero cost difference. The displayed six- 23
24   decimal endpoints are descriptive and do not decide a boundary test. Each interval is displayed on       24

25   two lines, with its lower endpoint above its upper endpoint; no endpoint digit or catalogue row          25

26   is omitted.   Every alternative previously admitted up to 2−12 is included in the                        26

27   singleton roster. The fixed pair-selection rule, including its deterministic cost-based                  27

28   ordering and tie handling, is part of the source lock. Its execution contributes no                      28

29   additional independent cost row or fit. The 24 model–tolerance records and 36 class                      29

30   comparisons use the already covered original-law cost family. Source, output and                         30

31   execution-clock hashes are retained with the exact multipliers, all 104 infeasibility                    31

32   certificates, and all 16,548 independently checked assignment inequalities.                              32

           APPENDIX AU: C OMPLETE PROSPECTIVE EVIDENCE AND VERIFICATION
14                                                                                                                14
                                                 BOUNDARIES
15                                                                                                                15

16     The fixed evaluation program reconstructs the R85 streams from the protocol and                            16

17   the nine unchanged acquired action tables. The source, bin arrays, raw endpoint                              17

18   arrays and exact integer moments have separate identities. Both arithmetic imple- 18
19   mentations cover every evaluated bin; midpoint checks supplement rather than                                 19

20   replace that full-bin calculation. A replay is not an additional independent sample. 20
21                                                                                                                21

22                      AU.1. Complete interval family and width components                                       22

23     For each of two streams, two estimators and 36 unordered pairs, the numerical                              23

24   tables report both directed endpoints and the exact decomposition                                            24

25                                                                                                                25

26
                                width = endpoint enclosure                                                        26

27                                         + sampling variance allowance                                          27

28                                                                                                                28
                                           + finite support allowance .
29                                                                                                                29

30   The endpoint enclosure combines original-law bin propagation, acquisition branch- 30
31   ing and directed arithmetic. It is not split into fictitiously independent subcompo- 31
32   nents. Acquisition ambiguity counts, integer rectangle evaluations, midpoint checks                          32
                           NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                     155

1    and endpoint-disagreement magnitudes are reported separately in the machine- 1
2    readable record. The cash and conditioned estimators use the same underlying rows   2

3    and are not independent replicates.                                                 3

26                                                                                                                26
                             AU.2. Selected-service price and fee breakpoints
27                                                                                                                27

28     Every comparison is selected minus comparator. If the upper cost endpoint is u,                            28

29   then the guaranteed extra-fee ceiling at zero resource price is −λu. With resource                           29

30   prices it becomes −λu − p · (wbj − wk ). Negative ceilings are retained: they do not                         30

31   support a positive fee based solely on policy quality. A declared normalized loss                            31

32   margin does not calibrate λ or a monetary fee.                                                               32

      of normalized loss, the sufficient extra-fee ceiling is minus the exact upper cost endpoint. Its display
14   rounds down. At general prices, subtract the priced resource difference and multiply the quality term by    14

15   the declared loss value. Negative ceilings remain in the table; the normalized units are not a monetary     15

16                                                  calibration.                                                 16

18                                                                                                               18
                         AU.3. Diagnostics and independently checked kernels
19                                                                                                               19

20     The new exact fixtures compare path enumeration with forward occupation re- 20
21   cursion, test the candidate-occupation counterexample, retain ties, reject impossible                       21

22   shared-parameter assignments, and exhibit a fractional outer bound that is valid but                        22

23   unattained. Separate tests check the ReLU/min-plus identity, label-noise enclosure,                         23

24   persistent-exploration recursion, sufficient screen closure, correct lower-versus- 24
25   upper work comparison, dated allocation, and price-region boundaries.                                       25

26     The frozen evaluation records zero new fits. Predictor warning rates, fallback                            26

27   frequencies and zero-progress query probes are not identified by that fact. Earlier                         27

28   fitting warnings and their affected services remain in the inherited experiment                             28

29   records. The R70 audit covers 188 service processes and records its prediction- 29
30   replay differences; it is not a 336-service manifest. The source-specific diagnostic                        30

31   index records these distinctions instead of assigning unsupported aggregate counts                          31

32   to the current evaluation.                                                                                  32
                              NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                        161

1      The large inherited vector descendant tree, a derivative branch-and-bound com- 1
2    parator, energy calibration, and a population experiment for a newly trained cross- 2
3    regime proposal require their own numerical evidence. Exact finite-state fixtures,        3

4    whole-bin evaluation of the nine attained controllers, or a successful PDF build do       4

5    not silently certify those different objects. This evidence distinction does not remove   5

6    the original algorithms, economic applications or full-horizon theorems from the          6

7    manuscript.                                                                               7

8                                                                                              8

9                                  AU.4. Publication identity                                  9

10                                                                                             10
       Current document hashes, source hashes, test counts, exact replay status and
11
     clean reconstruction status are recorded under the current revision’s audit directory. 11
12                                                                                             12
     A publication gate rejects absent current PDFs, bibliographies or source-matched
13                                                                                             13
     checks. Visual inspection is a separately signed-off observation against actual PDF
14                                                                                             14
     hashes; neither a compiler exit code nor an inherited release record is such an
15                                                                                             15
     observation. The full preservation edition retains every preceding active substantive
16                                                                                             16
     section. Its actual page count is reported rather than concealed by reducing type
17                                                                                             17
     size or deleting mathematical content.

                 8 Quadratic              61        67       0       67         0        4      0

21   No model: prediction-free service returns. Close: screen closures. Refine: protected refinements. Invalid   21

22   and repair count prediction diagnostics, not failed certificates. Zero counts probes improving neither      22

23   bound. Learned-model calls and dense arithmetic are separate from these events. Logical network             23

24   multiplication/addition/division counts are in the exact source-bound summary; interval-operation           24

25   counts and energy are not measured.                                                                         25

26     The disjoint action-value query account is in Table N.I; it is not a second addition                      26

27   of terminal work to a total that already includes it. The archived dense-model                              27

28   arithmetic is an implementation-level algebraic count. Peak resident memory is                              28

29   capacity. The timing observations are complete processes, not an estimated mapping                          29

30   from every operation to energy. Missing interval-operation or energy measurements                           30

31   remain missing rather than being assigned zero. Any cross-architecture numerical                            31

32   disagreement is retained by the reconstruction audit before the tables are accepted. 32

5                                                                                               5

6      The current reproduction command requires the ordinary article, technical sup- 6
7    plement, numerical appendix and response sources, rather than substituting an              7

8    older compiled article. It checks the preserved active input list, reconstructs the re- 8
9    quired tables, executes the selected mathematical and numerical checks, and records        9

10   their exact source and output identities. Each executed check has its own exit sta- 10
11   tus and log. A clean typesetting reconstruction removes inherited auxiliary and            11

12   bibliography outputs. The final release gate additionally requires a rendered-page         12

13   inspection record tied to the actual four PDF hashes.                                      13

17     The adversarial tests also retain atoms at a quantile, zero-probability outcomes,                     17

18   exact ties, malformed probability inputs, infeasible actions, negative or inconsis- 18
19   tent allowances, terminal error and correct date weights. A one-date singular-law                       19

20   example attains the signed discrepancy charge while its symmetric counterpart is                        20

21   twice as large. A two-date example rejects cancelling a discrepancy that is action                      21

22   independent only within each state. A four-outcome example separates static from                        22

23   nested tail risk. The tail-transport factor is attained as the risk level approaches                    23

24   one. These tests guard the original economic objective rather than selecting a more                     24

25   convenient replacement.                                                                                 25

       This section reports deterministic diagnostics of Section 8. They use finite rational
4                                                                                               4
     models with three states, two feasible actions and original nested average value at
5                                                                                               5
     risk. They are not additional investment observations, new fitting attempts, or a
6                                                                                               6
     rerun of the inherited largest-vector problem. The production calculation uses the
7                                                                                               7
     vertices of a capped probability simplex. The independent evaluator minimizes the
8                                                                                               8
     scalar threshold representation of average value at risk and performs a separately
9                                                                                               9
     coded original-value recursion. It does not import the centered-risk implementation.
10                                                                                              10
       The declared horizons are 2, 3, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, risk levels are 0, 1/2, 3/4, and the
11                                                                                              11
     four shared continuation parameters are −1, 0, 1, 2. At date t, state s ∈ {0, 1, 2} and
12                                                                                              12
     action a ∈ {0, 1}, transition weights are
13                                                                                              13

14          (1 + (t + s + a) mod 3,      1 + (2s + a) mod 4,      1 + (t + 2a) mod 3),          14

15                                                                                              15

16
     normalized by their sum. Costs are ((s + 2a + t) mod 5 − 2)/7 + a/5, the discount          16

17
     is 3/4, and the terminal vector is (0, 1/3, −1/4). These fully specified diagnostic        17

18
     primitives avoid a hidden change in objective between implementations. They are            18

19
     separate from the paper’s economic calibration.                                            19

20
       A single parameter determines the stored circuit throughout a configuration:             20

21                                                     θ+1                                      21
                          f t,θ (s) = ( T − t)θ/20 +       (s/2 − 1/2)+
22                                                      7                                       22

23                                       t mod 3                                                23
                                     +           (1/2 − s/2)+ .

                                            11                                                  24

26   uation, with smaller-action tie breaking. No training claim is attached to these           26

27   prescribed circuits. Residual intervals use radii 0, 1/64, 1/8 times (1 + s)/3; termi- 27
28   nal intervals use the same statewise radius. The exact configuration records retain        28

29   the complete input, every centered residual and risk penalty, lower and upper              29

30   potentials, a feasible maximizing risk occupation, and all strict kernel-exclusion         30

                           BA.3. Source, execution and release boundaries
19                                                                                                        19

20     The controlling advisory report is the R92 review deposited on 13 October 2026. 20
21   Its reviewed manuscript is the complete R92 article. The R94 development branch is                   21

22   a descendant of that manuscript; its paper entry point had not yet incorporated its                  22

23   new continuous-risk implementation. R95 retains the original title and author, adds                  23

24   the continuous primitive proof and executed comparison, and relocates fifteen com- 24
25   plete historical main-text sections to this appendix. Each moved file is byte-identical              25

26   to the R92 source. The only inherited scientific-section prose amendment clarifies                   26

27   that the earlier experiments use expectation, while the new explicitly specified ex- 27
28   periment uses nested AVaR. No original mathematical claim or adverse observation                     28

29   is removed.                                                                                          29

30     The cloud R94 attempt stopped before scientific execution when creation of its                     30

31   source branch was rejected. The available source-and-evidence artifact therefore                     31

32   contains sources but no completed primary run. R95 executed those sources locally. 32
                                NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: APPENDIX                                           177

1    A serialized actor and calibration hash was written before evaluation; it was not                               1

2    remotely timestamped or published before that local evaluation. The source checks                               2

3    in the evaluator reject changed models or changed frozen numerical files. Two tool- 3
4    interrupted local starts are retained as interrupted attempts, and the completed run                            4

5    is identified separately. Repeated evaluations of the same pseudorandom stream do                               5

6    not increase the number of independent observations.                                                            6

7      New-science tests, inherited-test replay, independent continuous-kernel valida- 7
8    tion, clean typesetting and actual-PDF inspection are separate release gates. The                               8

9    downloaded R92 publication artifact intentionally omits several older numerical                                 9

10   datasets and external historical code directories. Missing-fixture errors in replay of                          10

11   those inherited suites are recorded rather than skipped or reclassified as passed. The                          11

12   full repository materialization procedure preserves those historical dependencies;                              12

13   the local export is not evidence that their full replay has passed. The publication                             13

14   continuation restores the original repository dependencies and records its new                                  14

15   source-bound replay and delivery separately under audit/remote95. Historical                                    15

16   local-only receipts remain unchanged. An ARM64 execution, energy measurement                                    16

17   or full largest-vector replication is reported only with its own record.                                        17
